`ITenantRequestIsolationStore` is a technical facet of the existing tenant Session/Run owners. Its bounded inventory and metadata proof do not expose human-readable content or admit work. Human and Existing-Session Automation admission refuse another independent root when the Session or any prior admitted Run seals CurrentRequest. Trusted approval/configuration/background continuations retain their own causal validation. `ITenantRunStore.FindTenantAcceptedHumanInputAsync` compares the original principal/message key and exact accepted text, attachment IDs and interruption intent. `TenantRunService` returns only the factual receipt after current original-login/Session/Chat and source checks; stale old witnesses do not turn an exact retry into new work.

The existing tenant host implements `ITenantRequestIsolationControl` under its quiescent Session reservation after releasing the actual Run generation. `TenantRequestIsolationService` refuses nonterminal/uncertain Runs, pending input, incomplete bounded inventory, unsettled children or unaccounted completion receipts. It requests retirement of an exact existing Sandbox lease and waits for confirmation through the existing reconciler. `ITenantBrowserRetirement.RetireTenantIfBoundAsync` closes only an already owned view, repeating the canonical quiescent proof under native gates; a cold read creates no binding. The owning transaction repeats graph/revision/resource predicates before completing the Session and producing its existing lifecycle event. No completed Run regains an active write claim. Bounded deferred recovery uses the same host reservation and store proof, without another execution queue or conversation owner. Session working-file cleanup remains governed by its separate retention contract.

### Tenant User Profile ownership and self-service

A trusted User Profile is owned by the actual `(TenantId, User PrincipalId, AccountId)` membership link. The same Account has independent Profiles in different organizations. It is neither a global local-user Profile nor the Profile of the Session creator. A missing Profile is an empty revision-zero read, without an automatic seed or storage write. The closed preference fields are `preferredName`, `language`, `locale` and `timeZone`; only the original authenticated human may set/clear their own values, always with UserSet provenance. Explicit human text such as a preferred name of “friend” is valid; no historical demo-seed substitution applies.

`ITenantUserProfileStore` repeats actual live Account/login/tenant User/Principal checks within the incumbent IAM lock or SQLite transaction. Reads and writes take trusted original-human references, not a caller-selected Profile/owner ID. A write compares both tenant security revision and Profile revision, then commits the Profile, tenant revision and body-free audit together. Ownership is immutable and tied by a composite membership FK. Profile owners retain their actual User identity across security writes; suspension/removal uses the existing status tombstone, while detachment/reparenting is refused in both stores and guarded by SQLite. `TenantUserProfileService` fences returned private values against current original login, membership and changed witnesses after awaited reads/commit. Failed or uncertain save acknowledgement requires factual GET recovery, never automatic replay. This ownership/self-service boundary is the prerequisite for separately qualified model Profile evidence, Memory/continuity scopes and UI integration; it grants no executor read, model authority or permission to alter other members' preferences.

### Canonical Run Profile evidence

`ITenantRunProfileStore` is a separate trusted-Run read facet of the existing IAM/Profile owners. It never impersonates a human login or calls the self-service port. The actual stored UserMessage root supplies the original tenant User; Session creator, supplied owner IDs and independent Automation creators supply no Profile source. Current original User/Account/Principal, executor, source Session reads, Agent chat, raw executor `memory.read` and current operation policy are required. Passive recall withholds Review-required or unavailable data without creating/consuming consent. Admitted/current ProfileEvidence policy intersects before each new projection; an admitted exclusion cannot be broadened by later configuration.

A bounded `TenantProfileSource` witness (actual tenant/User/Account, Profile revision/time) accompanies the Run checkpoint before provider dispatch. Current values are reloaded before later model calls and replace the single Profile Evidence section. Disabling Profile evidence removes that section without rewriting stored preferences or trusted requester/authority facts. Already used source witnesses remain subject to current read permission and exact-version checks through checkpoint/output settlement; policy exclusion concerns new projection and does not erase historical tool evidence or promise a universal free-text filter. Source changes during a provider call refuse protected output instead of saving a newly unauthorized answer. Existing Session/Run persistence and transport owners repeat source checks; no new execution owner or global Profile adapter is introduced. Conversation readers retain the proposal's existing Session-authorized free-text history semantics and residual disclosure risk; raw Profile self-service remains private to its actual User.

### Canonical learned Memory ownership and outcome commits

`ITenantLearnedMemoryStore` is a trusted-Run facet of the existing IAM/Session/Run owners, separate from global Profile-based memory ports. Actual tenant User/Account/membership identity and the stored UserMessage root determine human ownership. Session facts are additionally partitioned by that User within the Session; IdentityUser facts bind that User and Agent; User facts bind that User within the organization across expressly enabled Agents. Independent Automation never adopts its creator's human memory. Unattended inferred facts can use their own Session namespace only. No model-supplied owner or global Profile ID establishes ownership.

`TenantMemoryService` refreshes optional Evidence before provider dispatch. Admitted/live MemoryPolicy flags intersect. Current independent executor/source-Agent `memory.read`, current requester/source context and current operation policy are required; passive Review-required recall is withheld without creating/consuming consent. Promotion creates an independent learned copy, preserving provenance without granting source-conversation access or deleting the long-term copy when that conversation is deleted. Both current consuming Agent and origin Agent read rights remain necessary. Historical memory receipts have a separate backend owner stamp; only the appropriate requester and current raw reader receives their resource projection.

Conversational intents remain proposals. The store prepares a bounded plan from actual scope owners, current `memory.manage`, configured scope promotion/retrieval flags and source facts. A plan grants no write. The existing atomic response-outcome transaction recomputes the plan under current authority/CAS, verifies exact private receipt ownership and commits the response, learned facts, source lineage, tenant revision and body-free audit together. Failed response persistence commits none of these learning changes. Existing bounds, trusted-field/secret rejection, superseding updates and open-loop resolution remain; conversational delete proposals require the separate exact-action forgetting path. Canonical forgetting/maintenance tools and human memory-management UI remain separate work.

Run checkpoints retain bounded immutable-value source witnesses before provider use. Existing provider/effect, checkpoint, new response/final outcome, factual report and recipient transport boundaries revalidate current raw source authority. New projections obey live exclusions; an already used value is not erased from historical model/tool data. Superseded/resolved facts retain their immutable source identity; deletion invalidates those witnesses. This is scoped source enforcement, not a universal free-text history filter.

### Canonical tenant working-file retention

`ITenantWorkingCleanupStore` reads bounded retained tenant Session/Run/causal-child/completion facts, including deletion tombstones. Terminal cleanup requires the latest admitted Run's immutable `WorkingCleanup.AfterTerminal` pin; an unused Session uses its creation pin. Committed deletion permits cleanup under either policy. Mere archive, completion under `OnSessionDeletion`, or later Instance configuration changes do not authorize removal. Active or uncertain effects, pending input, incomplete inventories and unaccounted causal completion defer cleanup. An exact owned Sandbox lease must be absent or confirmed Terminated.

`TenantWorkingCleanupService` uses this technical proof without a human/model path or new execution authority. `ITenantWorkingCleanupBytes` repeats the exact Session/policy-Run/lease witness under the existing filesystem provisioning gate before purging only the canonical owned Session tree. Home, sibling Sessions, Artifacts and retained metadata/receipts remain separate. Handle-based no-follow purge refuses substituted owner roots; cancellation/failure retains eligibility for retry. The existing five-minute workspace recovery worker scans at startup and advances bounded pages; cleanup is eventual, not part of the human lifecycle transaction. Runtime terminal persistence does not invoke a separate Profile-based cleanup actor; this exact tenant proof is the sole production working-file cleanup path. No migration or successful-cleanup row is required for idempotent physical convergence.


## Semantic recovery diagnostics

`ILanguageModel.InferenceTransport` optionally exposes the provider-neutral `ModelInferenceTransport`; Synthetic/custom models may return unknown. The semantic decorator forwards the inner value. `RunAttemptDiagnostic` adds optional `MaxOutputTokens` and `Transport`, describing the actual prepared request ceiling and configured transport rather than inferred token usage.

`ToolLoopExecutionState.ProtocolRecovery` stores bounded reason/channel, eligibility, strategy and outcome. Strategies are `terminalText` or `completionOnly`. Outcomes include `started`, `retryPending`, `succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled` and `interrupted`; terminal projection resolves an unfinished started record to failed/cancelled. Eligibility explains terminal, cancellation, deadline, uncertain effect, visible output and pending calls. The public Run diagnostic projection omits unrelated general provider-failure records from ProtocolRecovery while preserving FailedAttempts and the original checkpoint. Semantic channels, historical channel-less missing-display/invalid-block reasons, and actual correction strategies remain visible. No provider response body or prompt is retained. Initial BackgroundOutcome uses work.complete without a conversational envelope; see [backend recovery](12-backend-implementation-spec.md#bounded-semantic-response-recovery).

### Exact inline Artifact consent

`PreparedArtifactPublication` binds the actual parent Session and canonical bounded arguments. The existing prepared operation hash also binds Instance/persona witnesses. Semantic root correlation excludes witness revisions, preventing the same exact action from bypassing pending, rejected, cancelled, expired or already approved consent through a direct tool or refreshed witness. Model input cannot select identities, scope, permissions, grants or revisions.

Preparation, decision and effect repeat actual human-message lineage, owned Session/Run, admitted/current capability, raw executor `sessions.read` and current requester Session/chat access. Ordinary Review additionally requires raw executor creation authority; an ExceptionalGrant substitutes only for missing delegatable `artifacts.create`. Qualified humans need independent Session read plus creation and approval permissions on the exact Session, with current independence policy and grant authority where applicable. Inspection requires approval read, underlying creation permission and separate Session read; these filters apply before collection count/paging.

`TenantApprovedOperationService` dispatches this variant through the existing local effect port. Both owning stores repeat current source, witness, executor and reviewer checks and atomically save Artifact metadata, actual executing Run marker, immutable receipt, grant consume/revoke, security revision and audit. SQLite stages private bytes before metadata and rechecks expiry/claim after I/O. Failed transactions can leave unreachable private bytes; cleanup remains separate. Saved content is untrusted plain data, never a command. Publication supplies no read grant, role, rich reference or extra publication permission. [Verification](reports/tenant-artifact-review-verification-2026-10-18.md).

## Tenant Artifact reference and projection ports

`IArtifactReferenceAuthorizer.PrepareAsync` runs on the supervised model worker. The tenant binding requires an actual owned source, current claim/configuration, independent `sessions.read`/`artifacts.read`, and current Allowed read policy. Its frozen synchronous result is response-local evidence, never a durable grant. `TenantArtifactService.ProjectContentAsync` bounds source size and repeats original-human identity/security/Session/source fences after projection; Office preview reuses the existing processor. `TenantDeliveredBlock` carries exact saved block ID/kind/Artifact identity on the existing trusted receipt, bounded to 32. The store repeats actual source and current read access before credit.

## Tenant Artifact metadata verification ports

`TenantArtifactRuntimeReadService` converts existing `artifacts.verify` with one bounded exact `artifactId` argument. The actual Session comes from the trusted claimed Run. Only name, stored type/size/checksum, creation time and source identity are returned, with a Core-owned checkpoint witness. No bytes, workspace path, source Attachment ID, generic file list or new content-reader tool is added. Raw `sessions.read` and exact `artifacts.read` remain mandatory; either operation policy can require Review. `PreparedArtifactVerification` binds actual immutable source facts and parent Session, while the action hash also binds Instance/persona witnesses. The shared `verify_artifact` authorization intent prepares that exact action. A missing raw read cannot become an exceptional grant. Existing approval visibility filters both scopes and actual source before counts/paging; reviewer qualification repeats underlying reads, approval scope and independent-review policy. The local receipt records metadata consent, not a file mutation. Delivery repeats current source/claim/authority checks; retained direct and approved observations are checked before model/continuation reuse.

## Tenant model Office mutation contract

The four existing `office.document.create/edit` and `office.spreadsheet.create/edit` tools use the tenant workspace mutation owner, exact `mutate_workspace` consent, primary one-time write grant and Run/receipt recovery. Arguments are closed recursively against the incumbent Office tool schema, limited to 64 KiB UTF-8, and bind an exact matching DOCX/XLSX home or working path. Current independent Session/parent reads, admitted/current capability and HomeAccess apply; editing also requires ordinary workspace read authority, which a write grant cannot replace.

Create commits a new file exclusively and never replaces an occupied name. Edit reads at most the existing 10 MiB package ceiling, invokes the incumbent guarded semantic processor and compares exact original bytes immediately before native replacement. Current authority/claim/source checks surround processing and repeat at commit. Ordinary concurrent native writers retain existing filesystem semantics. Processing does not calculate formulas, publish an Artifact or grant output-read authority. An uncertain attempted effect is retained without automatic replay through the existing mutation disposition.

The shared approval inspector must expose the complete supplied title, sections, typed cells and ordered edit arguments as escaped data, with their exact path and tool. It must never project current private source bodies or the saved source witness. Approval remains separate from the recorded effect result. [Executed qualification](reports/tenant-office-mutation-verification-2026-10-19.md) distinguishes implementation, backend/browser evidence and remaining proposal scope.

## Tenant workspace Artifact source boundary

File publication uses exact native source bytes rather than embedding binary data in the inline UTF-8 publication contract. `ITenantWorkspaceArtifactSourceStore.CaptureArtifactSourceTenantAsync` is a facet of the existing canonical workspace adapter. It supplies a bounded captured source with owned root/path, source type/size/checksum and a private observation hash. `CopyContent` returns an independent byte copy; `RequireCurrentAsync` repeats the native source check. Capture and repeat validation use no-follow native traversal, exact link route and inode/change metadata plus streamed content hashing. Owner-root and parent identity/access metadata are bound without treating unrelated directory timestamp changes as source changes. No owner root is created on read. The configured file bound and existing 50 MiB workspace and Artifact ceilings all apply before allocation; this is distinct from the Office processor's 10 MiB processing ceiling because export copies bytes without interpreting the package.

A captured source is storage evidence only. It carries no permission, approval, Run claim, expiry, role or recipient-read grant. Its revalidation performs filesystem work only, without IAM/database reentry, so the existing Artifact transaction can combine that evidence with its own current authority/owner/claim checks under its incumbent lock/transaction. Publication must separately freeze and verify the captured bytes, preserve the saved exact source, and use the existing Artifact receipt and grant owners. Capturing a source does not expose a model publication tool or prove that full export conversion is implemented.

Native checks retain ordinary concurrent filesystem semantics; they are not a cross-process snapshot or lock. Capture and revalidation have [focused executed coverage](reports/tenant-workspace-artifact-source-verification-2026-10-19.md), followed by the internal transaction and exact backend consent qualification below. The successor [model/HTTP/browser qualification](reports/tenant-workspace-artifact-model-verification-2026-10-19.md) covers the offered conversion and shared inspector.

The source publication conversion extends the incumbent Artifact publication owner. Its closed source arguments are an exact absolute owned home/working `path`, sanitized `displayName` and optional valid `contentType`; tenant, Session, executor and authority remain Core-derived. A private source witness binds actual path/scope, inferred source type, size, checksum and native observation to an exact prepared action. Root correlation excludes that mutable witness, so changed bytes cannot bypass a previous refusal for the same source intent. Source reads require current independent Session/workspace reads, the home Agent read where applicable, admitted/current export capability and HomeAccess. The existing direct Artifact transaction combines its own current authority/Run/configuration checks with filesystem-only source revalidation and frozen bytes, then commits one Artifact/Run/receipt/security/audit result. Source staging grants neither later source reads nor recipient file access. Model offering uses `TenantWorkspaceArtifactContract.ForModel`, with actual capability and current human-message context required. `TenantToolAuthorization` routes the converted source call through this incumbent atomic publication service; the runtime does not also prepare a legacy side-effect fence. Source-policy Review returns a body-free `requiresApproval` result.

[Internal transaction qualification](reports/tenant-workspace-artifact-publication-store-verification-2026-10-19.md) records the direct path. [Backend source consent qualification](reports/tenant-workspace-artifact-consent-verification-2026-10-19.md) additionally covers durable Review/grants, raw scope filtering, real successor checkpoints, refusal suppression and transactional grant/receipt rollback; model offering and product journeys remain separate.

Source consent uses the same Artifact publication operation and parent Session scope. `PrepareWorkspaceArtifactAsync` captures the exact native source and combines source-read policies with the primary creation decision. A primary `artifacts.create` exception never substitutes for raw Session/home-Agent/workspace reads. Human inspection requires those independent reads; collection counts/paging filter them before projection. Qualified reviewers also need approval authority and independence on source scopes whose current policy requires Review. Preparation, decision and effect repeat source currency, current configuration and actual ownership. `TenantArtifactSourceTarget.RequireSavedAsync` verifies the saved source for pending and actual successor Runs. Approved execution materializes only that source through the existing Artifact transaction/receipt/grant owners; changed sources yield a denied factual result without adaptation or replay. Source materialization on `TenantOperationCommit` is ephemeral Application evidence; private source bytes/callbacks are never persisted in the approval or checkpoint. The closed `publish_workspace_artifact` intent matches this source variant only and supports exact successor continuation. Its capability-specific model schema and shared source inspector are qualified by the [successor report](reports/tenant-workspace-artifact-model-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant pinned resource source boundary

`TenantRunConfigurationResolver.CurrentResourcesAsync` resolves the actual tenant/Instance resource graph without Credential bindings or Sandbox readiness. Run admission reuses that same graph, then adds its existing independent Credential/Sandbox configuration. An internal `TenantResourceSourceTarget` resolves an exact admitted `/agent/resources/...` or `/agent/instance/resources/...` file against this current graph. It requires a current claimed conversational Run, current capability and independent Session, Agent and exact Instance-child `resources.read` permissions and policy. An inherited source also retains its exact admitted Definition/version. Current binding, resource revision, Run, security, Instance and Session fences repeat after byte I/O. A captured source is defensive data, never a permission, grant or durable consent.

`ITenantResourceSourceContentStore` is a read facet of the incumbent immutable Definition-resource blob store. The source target establishes ownership/IAM before calling it. Both adapters verify declared byte length and SHA-256; native reads use existing descriptor-relative primitives with no-follow opens, hard-link ownership checks, pre-allocation bounds and a bounded fresh source hash. Reads create no root or workspace marker. The resource ceiling remains 8 MiB. Ordinary concurrent filesystem semantics apply; this is not a cross-process lock. No new blob index, table or publication owner is introduced.

The incumbent `workspace.read`, `office.document.inspect` and `office.spreadsheet.inspect` consumers now use this source for exact admitted resource files. Namespace listing/search uses the collection variant below; other virtual resource families remain separate conversions; there is no unscoped resource fallback or new recipient-download permission.

`PreparedWorkspaceRead` has an optional canonical `ResourceKey` and `ResourceRevision` variant. It is read-only, uses primary `resources.read` on the actual Instance child, and retains independent `sessions.read` and `agents.read`. A Review binds the canonical tool arguments, immutable content observation and resource revision; raw reads remain required for both executor and human reviewer. No exceptional read grant is created. Current binding/revision and inherited Definition/version checks repeat during preparation, decision, the existing local consent receipt transaction, successor observation and retained-checkpoint use. Current resource ownership and all compound reads filter inbox counts before paging. Refusal suppression remains semantic within the existing root activation, including after policy relaxation.

Resource text remains strict UTF-8 untrusted data. Office reads reuse the incumbent bounded processor and pagination; neither reader changes a resource or grants file publication/download access. [Executed qualification](reports/tenant-resource-read-consent-verification-2026-10-19.md) distinguishes internal source, actual model/HTTP consent, SQLite recreation and unverified presentation/families.


## Tenant resource namespace observations

`workspace.list` and `workspace.search` accept exact `/agent/resources` and `/agent/instance/resources` roots or descendants. They select only resources that remain both admitted and current. Independent Session/Agent reads qualify the parent observation. Current raw child `resources.read` filters files before deriving folders, paging, scan bounds or results; unreadable files contribute no names, counts, cursors or truncation flags. Virtual paths remain literal manifest addresses, with no filesystem fallback or new directory/Environment IAM scope.

`PreparedWorkspaceRead.ResourceMembersJson` is a closed canonical, key-sorted array of child key/revision witnesses, omitted for home/working and exact resource-file observations. The collection is bound to its actual Session (`sessions.read` primary); every contributing child remains an independent compound read. Listing includes the bounded page and lookahead representatives; search includes scanned non-matches, bounded lookahead and any depth-limit witness. An empty readable namespace has no child authority and reveals no hidden-file existence. Parent policy can still require durable Review. The variant remains Review-only; no read permission or role is granted by consent.

Preparation, human access/reviewer qualification, approval counts, decision, local consent transaction, successor result and checkpoint reuse revalidate the exact member set, current child ownership and all reads. The immutable observation fingerprint binds negative matches and pagination without storing private names/snippets in the approval. A new visible set, changed source or revoked read invalidates the saved observation; semantic root refusal survives policy relaxation. Namespace results are untrusted bounded observations, not writable roots, exhaustive search or recipient download access. [Qualification](reports/tenant-resource-namespace-verification-2026-10-19.md).


## Tenant exact knowledge observations

`TenantKnowledgeReadContract` accepts only one bounded `identity`. `TenantResourceSourceTarget` derives its actual admitted/current Knowledge resource and requires independent current Session, Agent and exact child reads. Keyed identities retain exact Instance/Definition origin; friendly identities select only a unique admitted Definition source. Reference/Template/StaticAsset bodies cannot become knowledge merely by naming a path. Current references and resource metadata must still equal their pins. The returned identity/title/citation and strict UTF-8 text are bounded untrusted data, with no mutation, publication, later discovery or recipient download authority. Tenant retrieval never consults unscoped file/publication fallback.

The existing `PreparedWorkspaceRead` uses optional `Knowledge` metadata (identity, title, citation, virtual source path), omitted for other read variants. Canonical identity arguments, exact resource key/revision and the source observation bind its action hash. Read-only Review uses `resources.read` on the actual child plus independent Session/Agent reads; consent never substitutes for raw reads. Human inspection, counts, decision, the existing local consent transaction, successor result and checkpoint reuse retain current source/reviewer checks. Semantic root refusal binds the identity/tool/Session independently of mutable source witnesses. [Focused qualification](reports/tenant-knowledge-read-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant resource Artifact sources

`TenantArtifactSourceTarget` supports exact admitted resource files through the internal publication facet of `TenantResourceSourceTarget`. Export capability is independent of read-tool capability. Optional `PreparedArtifactSource.ResourceKey` / `ResourceRevision` identify the actual source child and are omitted for home/working witnesses. `TenantResourceRevisionRules` is shared by read consent and publication; stores validate current source revisions within existing transactions. Materializers carry verified defensive byte copies and perform storage-only checks, with no IAM/database reentry under publication locks. Source targets require raw Session, Agent and child `resources.read`; exceptional creation consent never grants those reads or recipient download access. [Focused qualification](reports/tenant-resource-artifact-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant Attachment intake and human reads

`ITenantAttachmentStore` resolves actual retained ownership, prepares bounded defensive byte copies, pages authorized live metadata and verifies exact content length/SHA-256. Structural child ownership includes expired metadata for grant retirement; public availability does not. `TenantAttachmentService` requires the original current login/Account/principal, actual Session/Agent and independent raw permissions, then repeats current authority/source checks after I/O or Office processing. Filtering precedes cursor/lookahead calculation, and cursors convey no authority.

`ITenantAttachmentMutationStore` participates in the existing canonical IAM lock/transaction. Upload binds a fresh request UUID, original human, exact owner, security/Session/Instance revisions, sanitized name, detected type, bytes and immutable hash. Same-request/same-payload retries return the original live identity under current authority; changed payload/actor conflicts. Aborting an unbound upload expires it and retires exact child assignments atomically with revision/audit. Archived/ended Sessions and inactive Agents deny writes while authorized retained reads remain possible. Delete requires independent child read and delete permissions.

`ITenantAttachmentCleanupStore` exposes only technical candidates derived from retained pending expiry or durable Session deletion. A target with an empty AttachmentId denotes the exact retained deleted Session root, including private failed-stage orphan bytes. `TenantAttachmentCleanupService` runs inside the incumbent supervised cleanup host, bounds scans, isolates target failures and retries without human/model deletion authority. It retains metadata and never adopts legacy roots. This boundary does not enable tenant message binding, attachment tools, processing/composer or Attachment publication. [Qualification](reports/tenant-attachment-storage-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant Attachment binding within message admission

`TenantRunService.SubmitMessageAsync` prepares at most ten distinct exact Attachment IDs alongside bounded text and optional normalized typed message parts (empty text is allowed only with files). `SubmitTextAsync` delegates with no files or structured parts. The [tenant Composer contract](14-api-and-realtime-protocol.md#structured-user-message-parts) governs current HTTP/Hub typed references, exact accepted-input identity and independent human/executor source reads. Trusted `TenantHumanRunAdmission.Attachments` holds frozen source witnesses; Core derives ordered immutable `ConversationAttachmentRef` values. No caller-selected labels, ownership, processing result or blob address is accepted as a wire field.

The incumbent `ITenantRunStore` admission verifies current original-human child reads and exact source owner/metadata/state/expiry within the same transaction/gates as the entry, Activation, Run, Session/security revision and audit. Only a live unbound pending source may bind to a new entry. Same-message retries compare ordered Attachment refs and text, return the original graph and add no binding/audit/revision; changed identity/payload or reuse by a new message refuses. Binding does no byte I/O or extraction and copies no human permissions to the Agent. Scoped input processing and Allowed-policy rereads are qualified below; public attachment messages remain closed pending their explicit provided-file handoff and transport integration.

`TenantConversationService` projects Attachment references through the canonical human Attachment read owner for the actual Session and a bound source. Metadata labels/types come from the actual source; model-selected response labels are removed and file blocks use generic `File`. Current independent child reads determine visibility, without granting content/download or executor authority. [Focused evidence](reports/tenant-attachment-binding-verification-2026-10-19.md).

The [scoped runtime input adapter](#tenant-attachment-runtime-input-context) now replaces the initial blanket dispatch refusal. Internal binding alone still grants no processing, content or read authority.

## Tenant Attachment runtime input context

`TenantAttachmentRuntimeContextService.PrepareAsync` accepts a trusted current Run and its owning snapshot, resolves current actual ownership/claim/lifecycle, and projects at most 50 authorized bound sources. Original entry identity, canonical source metadata and independent executor parent/child reads determine eligibility. Filtering precedes the source/manifest bound. Policy-required Review or denied reads reveal no source-derived name/ID/bytes; explicitly submitted current human selectors are preserved as described below, and the user task gets a generic unavailable-file notice. Current admitted user-entry sources supply processed input, while older bound refs supply metadata only. Durable snapshots remain unchanged.

Automatic processing of files supplied with the user turn is an input modality; it does not require or grant the optional `attachments.read` reread tool. The explicit read boundary below enforces that tool’s own admitted/current capability and exact operation authorization. Source content goes through the new frozen-source facet of the incumbent `IAttachmentProcessor`, with length/SHA verification, existing parser limits, guarded Office processing and image sanitization. A text-only model receives no image bytes; inert unsupported files have no automatic reader. No copy to `/working` occurs.

`TenantAttachmentContextSource` records bound entry/source identity, canonical name/type/size/SHA and optional processor version. A null processor version means metadata only. `RequireSourcesAsync` repeats current scope, claim, IAM and policy, compares exact metadata and verifies native bytes for processed sources. It runs after processing, immediately before provider dispatch and before checkpoint save. `TenantRunExecutionAuthority` validates stored witnesses before checkpoint reuse. Witnesses are private provenance, never role/permission snapshots; malformed, duplicate, oversized or mismatched sets refuse. The old blanket dispatch refusal is replaced by this scoped input path only. Public HTTP/Hub Attachment IDs and composer now use the common admission contract below; publication remains unconverted. Allowed-policy explicit reads and exact Review are qualified below. [Qualification](reports/tenant-attachment-context-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant Attachment explicit read boundary

`TenantAttachmentReadContract` closes the model arguments to one Attachment UUID and optional bounded DOCX `offset`/`limit` or XLSX `sheet`/`range`/`cursor`. Duplicate/unknown fields, wrong types, invalid bounds, mixed sparse cursor/range and format-inapplicable windows refuse. A read is authorized only for a current claimed conversational Run, actual bound same-Session source, admitted/current tool capability and independent parent/child IAM plus policy. `attachment_read_review_required` reads no source bytes and creates no implicit consent or grant.

`TenantAttachmentRuntimeContextService.InspectReadAsync` uses verified owned bytes, the incumbent strict UTF-8/PDF/image processing and `IOfficeProcessor`/`OfficeReadResults` paging owners. Office results retain coverage and continuation; image results carry sanitized request-local `ModelImageContent`; unsupported content and non-vision requests return truthful inert notices. Bounded typed results travel through `TenantToolAuthorization.ExecuteAttachmentAsync`, preserving parts instead of flattening them into text. No workspace alias or recipient download access is returned.

Private `TenantAttachmentReadObservation` holds canonical arguments and exact source metadata/hash/version inside the existing tool result/checkpoint. Current source checks repeat after I/O, before checkpoint save and before every retained use. Restored image results rehydrate fresh sanitized bytes under the same checks. Missing typed binding refuses retained Attachment tool transcripts. This direct read adapter does not itself enable public file messages, composer or publication; exact Review uses the separate consent integration below. [Evidence](reports/tenant-attachment-read-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant Attachment exact read Review contract

`TenantApprovalService.PrepareAttachmentReadAsync` resolves the trusted claimed Run and calls `TenantAttachmentRuntimeContextService.PrepareInspectionAsync` for one canonical `attachments.read` argument set. `PreparedAttachmentRead` binds Session/Attachment/entry, stored name/type/size/SHA, optional processor version, canonical arguments and pinned model vision capability. The exact action hash includes source/configuration witnesses; the root semantic identity binds Session and requested window without letting changed source metadata evade refusal. Preparation and human inspection perform no content read.

Either independent `sessions.read` or `attachments.read` operation policy can require Review. Agent raw reads, admitted/current tool capability and actual bound source remain prerequisites. Human metadata visibility requires independent parent/child reads plus approval visibility. Decisions also require current underlying approval scope and any independent reviewer. `TenantAttachmentReadApprovalVisibility` filters actual sources and both read scopes before counts/paging in both stores.

The existing approved-operation owner commits a Review-only local consent receipt, with no OperationGrant. Continuation stores `readAuthorized`, `applied: false` and nullable `attachmentObservation` in the existing authorization tool transcript. Consent and data availability remain distinct. Current source/decision/receipt/reviewer/Agent checks precede guarded bytes; unavailable content returns a truthful reason and no image parts. Stored approved observations require the same binding before reuse or sanitized image rehydration. No public attachment transport, composer or publication is enabled by this contract. [Focused qualification](reports/tenant-attachment-read-review-verification-2026-10-19.md).

## Tenant provided-file selectors and transport boundary

`TenantRunService.SubmitMessageAsync` is the common HTTP/SignalR admission owner for plain text plus up to ten explicit pending Attachment IDs, including file-only messages. Binding still uses the existing actual-source/current-human/CAS transaction. `RequireMessageAttachmentsAsync` rechecks current original-human parent/child reads for a cached file-message acknowledgement; factual dedupe grants no file access. Source permission failures use `attachment_unavailable`, leaving conversation access independent.

Prepared native user text preserves only hidden IDs explicitly submitted in current admitted human input, as untrusted selectors with no established access. It reveals no queried historical ID or canonical source metadata. Allowed source projection remains independent, and missing Agent raw read cannot become consent/grant. A tool-capable Agent can use the same closed `read_attachment` intent for a supplied ID requiring Review. Stored user text and source bindings are unchanged; later history cannot discover suppressed selectors from that earlier prepared projection.

## Attachment source facet for Artifact publication

`ITenantAttachmentArtifactSourceStore` is a storage-only facet of the incumbent Attachment stores. Capture resolves an actual bound same-Session original, returns the existing `ITenantWorkspaceArtifactSource` with explicit Attachment/entry provenance, and freezes verified raw bytes. Its `RequireCurrentAsync` checks only native immutable bytes; current metadata/ownership/IAM are enforced by the caller and atomic store owner. No parser, metadata catalog, public writable alias or new file owner is introduced.

`TenantArtifactSourceTarget` selects this facet only for a canonical exact `/attachments/{id}` path. `TenantArtifactSourceRules` binds Session scope and independent exact `attachments.read`, without home/workspace authority. `PreparedArtifactSource` and its observation append nullable AttachmentId/EntryId/AttachmentName with null omission, preserving prior source payloads. Review inventory filters current actual Attachment and Session reads before counts/pages, and the existing exact creation Review/grant must never supply missing raw source reads. Direct and approved publication share canonical source checks and the same atomic Artifact/Run/receipt owner.

## Exact Attachment virtual read adapter

`TenantAttachmentVirtualReadContract` validates exact Attachment selectors for Workspace text and Office file readers and maps them to canonical `TenantAttachmentReadArguments`. It accepts no directory, filename alias, caller ownership or mutation. `TenantAttachmentRuntimeContextService.VirtualArgumentsAsync` checks current bound-source ownership, both capabilities and reader/media compatibility before canonical consent preparation. `InspectVirtualAsync` reuses the existing guarded reader, post-I/O source fence and output bound, then adds private original-tool/path provenance. It returns text-only tool results; image/PDF inspection continues through `attachments.read`.

`TenantWorkspaceReadIntent.Matches` recognizes the exact normalized `AttachmentRead` operation in an incumbent `inspect_workspace` checkpoint. There is no new port, schema, file index, approval kind or effect owner. Canonical Attachment result validation also recognizes these virtual reader tool names and refuses malformed provenance or revoked current original-reader capability.


### Tenant Attachment namespace observations

`TenantWorkspaceRuntimeReadService` handles exact `/attachments` list/search through `ITenantAttachmentStore`, independently of HomeAccess and workspace/Agent metadata permissions. The original list/search capability and explicit Attachment reread capability must both be admitted and currently enabled. Actual current Session and independent child Attachment IAM apply. The store's `boundOnly` selection filters bound state and permitted IDs before paging/scanning; pending, foreign-Session and unreadable children do not contribute counts, cursors or bounds. Existing created-time/UUID paging supplies canonical UUID paths. Display names are labels, never aliases or physical paths.

`PreparedWorkspaceRead.AttachmentMembersJson` is an omitted-when-null, canonical ID-sorted bounded set of AttachmentId/EntryId/SHA-256 witnesses. It is exclusive of resource/knowledge witnesses and uses `sessions.read` primary scope with compound child reads. Exact Review reuses the existing approval, local consent receipt, successor and checkpoint owners. Source witnesses include bounded lookahead and search non-matches; no snippets or file bodies enter consent. Current source/ownership/capability/IAM and reviewer checks repeat before decisions, receipt commit and retained result delivery. Consent grants no role, independent file read or recipient download authority.


### Attachment context authority versus conversation revision

`TenantAttachmentRuntimeContextService` fences actual current Run revision/claim, tenant security revision, Instance revision, Session owner/mode/lifecycle/archive/deletion and bound original identity/integrity. It does not equate an assistant-progress save with an authority change merely because the Session snapshot revision advanced. Existing fresh independent IAM/policy/capability checks and exact source/byte checks remain required; this distinction does not retry a model request, reuse a stale permission snapshot or widen historical source discovery. The Runtime's model-preparation supervisor settles unexpected pre-provider faults through its existing mailbox failure owner.


### Human tenant materialization port

`TenantAttachmentMaterializationService` orchestrates immutable original reads and `ITenantWorkspaceMutationStore.TryWriteNewTenantAsync` through repeatable current-human/source guards. `ITenantAttachmentMutationStore.TryPublishMaterializationAsync` extends the incumbent IAM transaction owner: it validates the actual original HumanSession, current compound authority, exact source/checksum, Session/Instance/security witnesses and closed human Artifact provenance before staging bytes and atomically publishing metadata, security revision and authorization audit. It never synthesizes a Run. Nullable `ArtifactRecord.CreatedByPrincipalId` is provenance, not read authority. A Run-owned tenant Artifact must have no human/source/working provenance; a human materialization must have all three and no Run. Both stores retain equivalent ownership, quotas, cleanup and immutability.


The original-human materialization service also exposes a read-only `InspectAsync` projection containing current exact source name, security revision, writable-lifecycle state and compound-copy hint. Exact original Session/Attachment reads are required independently from working write/Artifact creation. Original identity/source/security/Instance checks repeat before release; POST never treats the hint as authority. [Wire owner](14-api-and-realtime-protocol.md#canonical-human-attachment-materialization).

## Tenant exact live input interruption

The queued-input port also accepts an explicit exact `TenantInputInterruption` naming the current owned Run and revision. Core records its canonical behavior/target witnesses in the new UserTurn Activation evidence; the new message owns a fresh original-human root and inherits no task authority or refusal lifetime. Current human Session read/Agent Chat and independent exact Run read/cancel permissions are required. Chat permission alone cannot interrupt. The target must still be the actual running claimed generation in this Session; an old target cannot select a newer response.

The existing admission transaction stages the captured response prefix as Interrupted (`userSteer`), cancels that Run, releases its completion accounting, revokes an available exact grant, binds selected files and admits the new user entry/Activation/Run, with one security revision and explicit admission/cancellation audits. Any stale authority/source or invalid input refuses the whole change. Same-message retry includes behavior/target evidence and returns the original fact without stopping later work. While the interruption write is pending, the mailbox holds current normalized model/brain output with existing producer acknowledgements providing backpressure. Refusal resumes that output; success discards it and tombstones the response before cancelling workers. Accepted work and generation retirement remain supervised after the caller disconnects. The returned commit includes the exact cancelled Run, without a postcommit authority reload. Queueing and Stop retain their separate meanings. Qualification is recorded in the [interruption report](reports/tenant-input-interruption-verification-2026-10-21.md).


## Tenant Automation editor projection

`TenantAutomationService.EditorAsync` returns a current owned Agent summary, effective Schedule policy/eligibility, filtered paged registrations, exact per-row update/cancel/run hints, create eligibility and a security revision. Exact detail repeats independent Automation read and rejects another Instance route. Counts/paging use the same pre-filtered collection owner; hints add no authority. Archived Instances remain readable with mutation hints false. Existing save/cancel/manual-occurrence commands reauthorize their current source and exact revisions. [UI qualification](reports/tenant-automation-ui-verification-2026-10-21.md).


`TenantAutomationPresetRules` projects three immutable metadata recipes through that same editor read. It derives advisory prerequisites from the selected owned Agent, active Definition, current isolation/maintenance settings and unattended model catalog; it never calls a legacy Profile-owned catalog service. Creation accepts a known exact preset ID/version pair or neither. Omitted/null update metadata preserves the original pair, and relabeling or attaching a recipe to a custom registration refuses. Both owning stores repeat creation validation and existing immutable-provenance checks. Recipe eligibility grants no creation, activation, inspection, tool or approval authority. Disabled updates may retain an unchanged unavailable saved model; selecting a different model or enabling requires current selectability. An enabled event Automation needs at least one enabled child.

## Automation occurrence admission consent

Policy-required Review and a primary-only exceptional grant precede protected Session/Run creation. The existing trigger occurrence may reserve one exact source slot in AwaitingDurableWork while the canonical approval request is pending. It has no execution Session/Run, live generation or provider work. Advancing the registration, freezing the actual scheduled-null/manual-human origin, planned identities/model/configuration and creating the request are one IAM/occurrence/approval transaction. The request uses a closed Automation-admission source shape; ordinary approvals retain their actual Run/Session foreign-key requirements.

The ordinary approval inbox, reviewer identity, independent review, exact action hash, limits, expiry and processed signal remain authoritative. A single exceptional permission substitutes only its primary compound target; all other admission permissions and raw source reads remain independent. Decision and later atomic settlement repeat current source/configuration, requester, executor and reviewer/grant checks. Approval admits the exact reserved graph once; refusal, expiry, cancellation or changed authority settles the occurrence without a Session/Run. Changed intent cannot retarget old consent. No Task, extra queue or parallel execution owner is introduced. [Focused qualification](reports/tenant-automation-admission-verification-2026-10-21.md) records both stores, actual Runtime/cold-host execution and the shared browser flow; the full proposal remains open.

## Tenant webhook connections and receipt admission

`ITenantWebhookStore` is a facet of the existing IAM and WebhookEvent/ExternalEvent owners. Human create/rename/rotate/revoke operations repeat original-session, current scoped read/manage, source revision and security revision inside the same transaction as the management audit. Incoming tokens are bounded source credentials, not IAM principals: receipt provenance records the actual tenant/source and authenticated source revision without inventing a human or Agent actor. Admission repeats current source token and active tenant atomically; exact source-event replay returns its original factual receipt and changed evidence conflicts. No Run, subscription, background queue or execution grant is inferred from receipt acceptance. The source writer derives its own tenant revision and accepts no caller IAM child graph. Only the source, management audit and tenant revision may change. Built-in catalog metadata has independent current `core-events.read`. [Focused qualification](reports/tenant-webhook-verification-2026-10-21.md) records both stores, SQLite recreation, migration guards and native browser flows; Core/Webhook subscriptions and event admission have separate qualification below; canonical Email-watch conversion is qualified in the Email-watch ownership section below.


## Tenant event processing and Activity

`ITenantEventStore` is a facet of the incumbent IAM/Core/External event owners, covering immutable receipt-time subscription snapshots, source-filtered reads, durable predicate decisions, bounded buckets, exact source reservations and occurrence admission. `TenantEventProcessor` runs in the existing trigger worker, paging pending sources before due buckets. It reuses the bounded filter evaluator and source packing; no additional queue or Task entity exists. `TenantEventAdmissionRules` and `TenantAutomationOperationTarget` reconstruct exact receipt/batch ownership, source hashes and saved child/revision before every preparation/settlement transaction. Current executor raw reads remain mandatory and may require Review; a creation exception grants none of those reads.

`TenantEventReadAuthority` follows actual accepted occurrence lineage before model/effects and terminal completion, including NoAction. Retained accepted work survives later source token rotation/revocation subject to current independent raw reads. Actual Core producers derive closed metadata and causal root from committed tenant Run/Session/Instance owners. Canonical Email-watch production uses the owned source conversion and current fences described below.

`TenantCoreEventReadService` applies exact raw fact reads independently of opening a Run or Agent; collections additionally require their Agent read. `TenantEventActivityService` requires current Agent/Automation read, supplies raw-source visibility to the owning store before counts/paging, and projects only individually readable receipt data. Partial Core batch visibility hides the batch count, included IDs and linked work/consent. Run/Session/approval references require their independent current read checks. A final current-human/security revision check surrounds all dependent reads. Editor event capability hints are advisory and separate from IAM.


[Focused qualification](reports/tenant-event-automation-verification-2026-10-21.md) records the completed local Core/Webhook event module and its remaining proposal boundaries.

### Tenant-owned email monitoring source

`ITenantEmailWatchStore` extends the incumbent watch/hidden-proof owners through the tenant IAM store. Human configure/check repeats the original login, current credential permissions, security revision, credential logical revision and watch configuration revision in the owning commit. Poll claims belong to the enabled tenant/account source; they do not retain the configuring person's login or fabricate an executing Agent. Cursor success derives current active bindings, actual executor identities, raw source permissions, Core trigger policy and complete receipt-time subscriptions inside the source commit.

`ITenantEmailChangeFeed` exposes only bounded cursor/reference batches, source recheck and protected OAuth-maintenance callbacks. Infrastructure keeps Gmail/IMAP DTOs and secret handling. Baselines skip historical mail; gaps rebaseline explicitly. A completed scan advances the cursor and writes its metadata facts and hidden binding/lease/account proofs atomically. Runtime delivery and retained-read checks are distinct: watch-off fences unadmitted input, while admitted work retains current original-account/binding/raw-read/Review fences. These ports reuse Automation, occurrence, consent, Session and Run owners.

## Configured Automation report source proof

`TenantBackgroundCompletion.DestinationOccurrence` is read from the owning store for an independent Automation source. It must identify the accepted actual occurrence, its execution Session and initial Run, exact tenant/Instance/executor, Background target and frozen completion conversation. A caller-supplied registration or arbitrary Session ID is insufficient. Immediate-background children retain their incumbent actual parent proof. `TenantCompletionDestinationRules` composes exact destination eligibility and current `sessions.read`/`agents.chat`; these secondary permissions never become exceptional Run-creation grants. The owning stores repeat destination/source and consent checks inside final output transactions. Prepared admission consent uses closed schema 3 for configured destinations; historical one-Schedule bytes and schema-2 no-report event bytes remain unchanged.

### Existing-conversation Automation admission

`TenantAutomationChange.TargetSessionId` selects an existing conversation; null selects independent Background execution. It cannot combine with `ReportToSessionId`. `TenantAutomationConversationRules` owns target eligibility, conversation-model binding, typed input boundaries and actual accepted-occurrence communication checks. `TenantAutomationAdmission.ExpectedSessionRevision` lets both owning stores re-read the unchanged target snapshot before admitting only Activation/Run rows. Busy or pending-input targets reject direct admission; approved busy signals remain pending.

Existing targets compose `automations.run`, `agent-runs.create`, exact target `sessions.read` and Instance `agents.chat`; they require no `sessions.create`. Missing secondary read/Chat permission never becomes an exceptional grant. Approval visibility applies these current independent reads before count and paging. Closed prepared-admission schema 4 binds the target in Current/Next/occurrence plus `PreparedAutomationConversation(SessionRevision, InputBoundarySequence)`. Versionless, schema-2 and schema-3 serialization retain their historical contracts. The Runtime projection and both store readers share the frozen typed boundary, including protected-operation successors.

### Authorization audit queries

`IAuthorizationAuditQueryStore.QueryAuditAsync(Guid? tenantId, AuthorizationAuditQuery, CancellationToken)` returns `AuthorizationAuditPage(Items,Total,Page,PageSize)`. `AuthorizationAuditQuery` carries zero-based page, bounded size, exact action/decision/actor and inclusive From/Through. Null tenant means only the closed safe System fact set. Both adapters apply filters before count and page with At/AuditId descending order; SQLite uses one read transaction for page/count coherence. The application service normalizes filters, checks original-human authority around I/O and rejects changed tenant revision. It neither appends audit nor creates execution authority. [HTTP owner](14-api-and-realtime-protocol.md#canonical-audit-query-contract).

### System administration conversion contract

The canonical System module uses original-human `SystemAdministrationService` and a typed `ISystemAdministrationStore` facet for metadata-only Account/organization catalogues, organization creation and suspension/reactivation, and Account status. Catalogues check original login/permission before and after I/O; they expose no membership graph, execution/private resources, password hash or login stamp. Account versions are opaque hashes of current Account identity/status/stamp, used only as concurrency witnesses. Account status requires that version; organization status requires current tenant revision. Creation makes only the acting Account the new Owner. Both adapters repeat original-login/raw System authority inside each atomic command/audit transaction. Account last-active-Owner checks retain their existing owner. Tenant status supports Active/Suspended; it does not purge or remove a tenant. The [verification record](reports/system-administration-verification-2026-10-21.md) owns executed qualification; the full proposal remains open.

Canonical System Account status additionally preserves at least one active Account backing the built-in Platform Operator role. This check shares the status/audit transaction and applies even when the target is no longer an organization Owner; it closes the one-use-bootstrap lockout path. Refusal is409 `last_system_operator`, with no stamp/status/audit change. Tenant last-Owner protection remains independent. This does not add System role assignment or recovery authority.


## System operator store facet

`ISystemOperatorStore.QuerySystemOperatorsAsync` returns bounded safe Account/role metadata after literal filters and before paging. `TrySetSystemOperatorAsync` accepts trusted original-human references, exact Account version and enabled intent; actual stores repeat current governance authority and atomically settle role, rotated Account stamp and audit. `SystemOperatorService` repeats read authority before/after I/O. This is an existing built-in role assignment, not a general System role editor.


## Shared speech input stream resource

`SpeechRecognitionIngress` owns one opened `ISpeechRecognitionSession`, fresh stream ID, bounded PCM/control queue, sequence/offset validation and bounded deferred boundary markers. It copies admitted PCM into transport-owned memory and emits only normalized speech evidence/faults. It is not a conversation controller, human ingress authority or Run-admission API. The owning Session Runtime still owns mode, mute, utterance/controller decisions and conversational writes. Raw PCM stays outside the domain mailbox and persistence. Stop retires callbacks before provider cleanup, including late non-cooperative recognizers; provider-worker exceptions are supervised with safe diagnostics. Muting retires the provider stream; unmute opens a fresh one with a fresh ID/epoch, preserving independent playback. This shared resource does not itself enable tenant Voice transport or relax tenant Run-frame fences.


## Canonical tenant voice lease boundary

`TenantVoiceLease` is minted only by the authenticated Application voice use case and held by the server attachment owner. Its immutable tenant/Session/executor/original-human/attachment references and per-input stream tokens are absent from model arguments and stored credentials. Retirement closes ingress immediately and serializes with already acquired owning operations; transactions use an active operation guard for recognized speech. `TenantVoiceModeChange` activates only through `ITenantVoiceModeStore` under the host's quiescent Session reservation, with current original-human IAM and exact revision checks. Retirement can only downgrade the retired lease's own Session to Text. `TenantSpeechAdmission` marks a trusted recognized utterance on the existing human-input admission, retaining exact replay, current cancellation checks and fresh actual-human roots. Runtime bindings emit only typed turn/interruption/unavailable signals for supervised continuation outside the mailbox. The input token rotates on unmute/recovery under the same root lease. Playback uses an active root operation even while input is muted; speech admission requires a held operation for the exact enabled input token. `TenantRunService.SubmitSpeechAsync` reuses original-human admission and returns the committed entry sequence. `TenantPlaybackInput` carries server-derived actual-attachment delivery bounds; Runtime computes conservative heard credit and commits a typed `TenantHeardReceipt` before advancing playback counters or completing output. Display receipts also support Voice entries, independently of hearing. No public endpoint accepts a claimed server-recognition final. Qualification is recorded in the [tenant voice report](reports/tenant-voice-verification-2026-10-21.md); it does not complete the proposal.


## Human learned Memory management

`ITenantMemoryManagementStore` is the original-human facet of the same learned-fact owner. Lists and exact inspection bind the actual tenant User/Account/membership; Session reads additionally require the exact readable Session and Agent. User-wide facts remain independently filtered by each origin Agent's raw `agents.read` and `memory.read` before ordering, pagination and counts. Read/manage are independent. Authorized humans can inspect and forget retained facts even when model recall is disabled; no Runtime policy or model consent grants human authority. Independent agent Session facts require that readable Session and do not adopt a human owner.

`TenantMemoryForget` binds the current security revision and either one exact Memory/revision or the entire readable scope's retained count. The owning transaction repeats original login, ownership, raw reads and raw management of every selected origin. A scope reset refuses if any readable retained fact lacks management; it never partially skips such facts. Both stores tombstone values while retaining immutable identity, provenance and hashes; fact revisions, tenant revision and body-free audit commit together. The SQL reset uses an indexed scoped set update without loading historical private bodies. Every canonical fact mutation advances the tenant security revision, fencing the count/selection witness. Other scopes, promoted copies, other Users, source conversations, Experience and files remain independently retained. Lost/stale responses require explicit current readback and a deliberate new decision; they do not replay a command. Protected model forgetting/consolidation now follows the exact consent contract below; autonomous maintenance and broader continuity remain separate work.


## Protected model learned Memory operations

Canonical tenant tasks use `ITenantMemoryOperationStore` and `TenantMemoryOperationService` for `memory.forget` and `memory.consolidate`. The canonical continuity facade below owns `continuity.search`/`continuity.get` and composes the same store for its learned-Memory portion; retained typed Memory-only read requests/receipts remain readable. Memory operations remain Core-projected tools under pinned and current Memory policy. They accept no tenant, User, Account, Profile or owner selector. The owning store resolves the actual causal UserMessage root; independent Automation never borrows its creator’s private Memory. Reads require independent current Session, Agent and per-origin Memory reads before private body lookup. Primary manage authority may receive an exact exceptional grant; raw reads and secondary origin management cannot.

`PreparedMemoryOperation` freezes canonical arguments, exact IDs/revisions/content hashes, root Session/User and source labels in the incumbent prepared-operation/approval/Run path. Reads follow ordinary compound policy and return no values before required Review. A completed exact same-task read receipt can authorize later inspection of those same facts while current executor/reviewer reads and independence remain valid. This does not authorize another query result, another task, another User or mutation. Read witnesses, including metadata-only lookahead/tombstones, remain in checkpoint state through compaction and are checked before provider, output and report delivery.

Forget and consolidation always require exact consent. Forget tombstones one exact retained fact, including superseded/resolved facts; it does not reset a scope. Consolidation accepts two to eight active same-owner/scope/kind facts, validates a bounded replacement, supersedes the sources and adds one fact with immutable immediate-parent IDs in the same receipt/security/Run transaction. The result ID is deterministic for the tenant, actual owner and canonical source/replacement arguments, preventing repeated cross-Agent User-scope application. Where current read authority permits it, same-task replay reports the factual result without another effect. Current source changes, lost reads or invalid reviewers refuse or record denial without partial mutation. A completed same-task exact forget permits the task to explain its own observed deletion; external deletion invalidates historical private-body witnesses. Optional completion learning is suppressed after successful same-task maintenance to prevent silent re-creation/promotion. Other copies/scopes, conversations, approval history, Profile, Experience and files remain separately retained.

Human review requires the actual private User owner and current source reads/manage, plus approval rights. An independent-review policy with no eligible owner reviewer refuses rather than exposing another User’s facts. The review shows complete current readable source values and the exact replacement; source labels in retained approval history remain separately retained. Historical Session continuity is owned by the following boundary; Experience and autonomous maintenance remain outside this focused Memory conversion.

## Canonical historical continuity read boundary

`ITenantContinuityStore` and `TenantContinuityReadService` extend the existing read projection and exact approval/Run owners; it creates no history store, retrospective worker or new conversation writer. `PreparedContinuityRead` freezes the exact query/window, actual task root, owned Memory references and historical Session/revision/cursor/hash witnesses. Current same-Agent historical Sessions are independently filtered by raw Session/Agent/Chat access before bodies and ranking; the current Session is excluded. Historical retained conversation data remains distinct from learned Memory and private Profile rows. Retained statements are untrusted historical observations, not current authority. Search combines currently readable Memory and historical Sessions under bounded lexical ranking; exact get supports Memory or a bounded Session entry page. Experience joins only after its own canonical store/source conversion.

Required compound read Review uses the existing durable authorization substrate; it grants no raw permission, mutation or access to a substituted source. Current requester/executor/reviewer/source and admitted/current continuity ceilings must survive at observation, consent, provider, output and report boundaries. Source retirement, deletion, changed observed revisions or revoked reads invalidate witnesses rather than restoring old content. Human review and minimal status independently filter current source visibility before paging; no other tenant or unrelated Agent source is discovered from a supplied ID.

## Canonical Experience ownership and source intent

The canonical Experience facet uses actual Tenant/Agent/executor and causal User ownership, not a caller-selected Profile. A source observation contains a typed Session/AgentRun identity, stable cursor, exact bounded range, current source revisions and projection hash. Its model arguments contain only source selectors; Core derives ownership, source hashes and authority from the executing claim. `experience.record` requires a source previously inspected in that task and the unchanged cursor. Literal structured observations remain untrusted derived data. Required source Review and protected record/maintenance consent use the existing prepared-operation, decision, receipt and continuation owners; no raw-read grant is introduced.

Experience facts preserve the existing Eligible/Suppressed/Deleted/Superseded meaning and immutable content/source lineage. Human-root facts remain private to the actual User within the Agent; unattended Instance facts have no adopted User. Normal model recall excludes suppressed, deleted and superseded records. Human management remains independently permission-controlled even when model recall is disabled. Forget/reset removes Experience content without erasing source conversations, Memory, Profile, files or approval metadata. Conversion qualification and remaining parity are reported separately from the complete proposal audit.

## Explicit human Experience review admission

`ITenantExperienceReviewStore` is a narrow facet of the existing IAM/Session/Run owner. An original human can request a generic manual background review of one current-readable stable Session or substantive terminal Run. Core derives the source witness, actual User and deterministic graph identities. The source must belong to the selected Agent/executor; private Run evidence requires that same actual User. Current source reads, chat and both Session/Run creation permissions remain independent. The owning transaction repeats original-login, source, opt-in, Agent revision and security CAS before committing Session, input, Activation, Run and body-free audit together. No empty Experience row, separate job/outbox, scheduler or permission snapshot is created.

The review is explicit human input: its trusted root remains UserMessage/UserTurn, with a ManualBackground Session and BackgroundWork surface. This preserves actual private User ownership without changing independent Automation ManualInvocation attribution. The queued Run uses its normal pinned configuration/model/budget and protected tools; requesting review grants no source-read consent or recording permission. Repeated requests for the unchanged actual source/User return the same factual graph rather than replaying it. Source changes require a fresh observation; recording still requires actual same-task inspection and exact consent when current policy requires it. Implementation qualification is tracked separately from this contract.

Run source privacy is derived from validated retained evidence, not the mere presence of an Experience fact reference or an Automation creator/invoker. Stores resolve fact owner metadata under the source-validation transaction; Instance-only facts remain shareable under current raw reads. Private Profile/learned Memory, private Experience facts and private source witnesses require the same actual causal User. A derived Run preserves a private source witness even when it has no direct private fact/Profile/Memory read. Source classification never substitutes for current source-proof authorization.

## Tenant event Experience coverage

`ITenantEventStore.ListTenantEventCoverageAsync` reads bounded pending source coverage from the existing owned bucket/coverage rows. The original human must independently read the selected Agent and Automation; per-source Core/Webhook and monitored-account reads filter evidence. The reader repeats original-login/security fences after I/O. A cursor may return no visible items with continuation; that is not exhaustion. No legacy Profile facet can read or acknowledge tenant rows.

The Runtime checkpoint owner derives acknowledgement only from validated retained `TenantExperienceSourceReference` witnesses and the actual accepted Core-event occurrence/root/bucket/child. Only represented `run.completed`/`run.failed` source Run IDs are acknowledged, within that exact bucket. Attempted/denied/over-budget reads, unrelated Runs, other children, webhook-supplied Run IDs and mere receipt of an event do not establish inspection. Successful source inspection need not record Experience. The existing Run transaction commits checkpoint and coverage together; approval successors resolve the actual original event root. Acknowledgement grants no read or record authority and does not remove immutable event history.
